# Relatório gerado da progressão

Gerado por `smp-profissoes/tools/gerar.py` a partir dos jars instalados. Não edite à mão.

## Mod inicial (escolhido no menu de profissão)

- **Engenheiro**: Create (`eng_create`) | Immersive Engineering (`eng_ie`) | Embers (`eng_embers`)
- **Arcanista**: Iron's Spells (`arc_irons`) | Malum (`arc_malum`) | Botania (`arc_botania`) | Neo Vitae (`arc_neovitae`)
- **Ferreiro**: Silent Gear + Productive Metalworks (`fer_silentgear`) | Forbidden Arcanus (`fer_arcanus`)
- **Naturalista**: Farmer's Delight + Farm & Charm (`nat_cozinha`) | Herbalismo (`nat_ervas`)

## Componentes (pesquisas cruzadas)

- **Ballistic Engineering** (`smpprofissoes:oficios/engenharia_balistica`) — level 4, parent: Immersive Arsenal; buildings: Mechanic's Hut 4, Blacksmith's Hut 3; cost: 32x immersiveengineering:ingot_steel, 8x silentgear:crimson_steel_ingot, 32x minecraft:gunpowder
- **Electromagnetic Railgun** (`smpprofissoes:oficios/railgun`) — level 5, parent: Ballistic Engineering; buildings: Mechanic's Hut 5, Blacksmith's Hut 4; cost: 4x immersiveengineering:capacitor_hv, 16x silentgear:crimson_steel_ingot, 2x minecraft:netherite_ingot
- **Runeforged Alloys** (`smpprofissoes:oficios/ligas_runicas`) — level 4, parent: Master Toolsmith; buildings: Blacksmith's Hut 4, Enchanter's Tower 2; cost: 8x malum:soul_stained_steel_ingot, 8x silentgear:azure_electrum_ingot, 4x minecraft:netherite_scrap
- **Herbal Inks** (`smpprofissoes:oficios/tintas_herbais`) — level 4, parent: Arcane Mastery; buildings: Enchanter's Tower 4, Plantation 3; cost: 64x irons_spellbooks:arcane_essence, 16x toxony:nightshade, 16x herbalbrews:lavender, 16x botania:mana_powder
- **Legendary Artifacts** (`smpprofissoes:oficios/grimorios_lendarios`) — level 5, parent: Herbal Inks; buildings: Enchanter's Tower 5; cost: 4x irons_spellbooks:legendary_ink, 2x hazennstuff:deus_essence, 1x minecraft:nether_star
- **Venomous Alchemy** (`smpprofissoes:oficios/alquimia_venenosa`) — level 4, parent: Natural Alchemy; buildings: Plantation 4, Enchanter's Tower 3; cost: 16x toxony:toxin, 16x malum:wicked_spirit, 4x minecraft:wither_rose
- **Eternal Brews** (`smpprofissoes:oficios/pocoes_eternas`) — level 5, parent: Venomous Alchemy; buildings: Plantation 5, Enchanter's Tower 3; cost: 1x minecraft:nether_star, 64x toxony:toxin, 8x botania:mana_diamond
- **Spellforged Arms** (`smpprofissoes:oficios/armas_encantadas`) — level 4, parent: Eldritch Depths; buildings: Enchanter's Tower 4, Blacksmith's Hut 3, Plantation 1; cost: 1x minecraft:nether_star, 8x minecraft:echo_shard
- **Stellar Forging** (`smpprofissoes:oficios/forja_estelar`) — level 5, parent: Runeforged Alloys; buildings: Blacksmith's Hut 5, Enchanter's Tower 4, Plantation 3; cost: 2x minecraft:nether_star, 8x forbidden_arcanus:stellarite_piece, 4x minecraft:dragon_breath
- **Terrasteel Synthesis** (`smpprofissoes:oficios/sintese_terrasteel`) — level 4, parent: Natural Alchemy & Runes; buildings: Enchanter's Tower 4, Plantation 3, Blacksmith's Hut 3; cost: 16x botania:mana_diamond, 1x minecraft:heart_of_the_sea, 1x minecraft:totem_of_undying
- **Alfheim & the Gaia Trials** (`smpprofissoes:oficios/portal_alfheim`) — level 5, parent: Terrasteel Synthesis; buildings: Enchanter's Tower 5, Plantation 4; cost: 4x botania:terrasteel_ingot, 16x botania:mana_diamond, 64x botania:livingwood
- **The Ember Blade** (`smpprofissoes:oficios/lamina_ember`) — level 5, parent: Runeforged Alloys; buildings: Mechanic's Hut 5, Blacksmith's Hut 4, Enchanter's Tower 3; cost: 16x embers:dawnstone_ingot, 2x minecraft:netherite_ingot, 8x minecraft:echo_shard

## Etapas

Itens **explícitos** têm vedação total (máquinas, autocraft e saque). Mods inteiros (`resto_de`) são liberados na bancada e nas máquinas desses mods, que só existem depois da pesquisa.
